# N6 exact hull: MIXED fixed case appendix

Read this appendix with N6_EXACT_HULL.md. Each node inherits its parent’s
signs, adds exactly its printed assumptions, and applies the listed
elementary deductions in order. All leaf products and weights are printed.
The appendix contains no external certificate or discovery-data premise.
